HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001","root_001033/B005","root_001033/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_00c7c869952a5b79bd39","text":"1. **Following as ordered succession, later mirrored by consequence.** Lexical evidence: ت ل و B001 \"following in sequence\"; ع ق ب B005 \"coming after in succession\" and B006 \"the end and outcome\" in 91:15. Structural cue: moon follows sun near the beginning; outcome follows deed at the end. Assigned role: تلا supplies the orderly secondness that عُقبى later moralizes. Abductive move: I supply the analogy from celestial succession to moral succession; the packet supplies both follow-roots and final consequence. Reading change: the moon's following becomes an early model of a universe where things come after one another lawfully, making the last aftermath less sudden.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001255/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0ba5c367246f1c29d59b","text":"3. **Moonlight as a remainder of what precedes.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ق م ر/B001` includes lunar light, and fixed `ت ل و/B003` is a remainder that follows what came before. **Structural cue and role:** the repeated suffix `-ها` across 91:1-4 keeps one relational field in view while manifestation and covering alternately act on it; the remainder branch lets lunar light occupy the residual phase. **Abductive move:** the shared suffix and sequence are supplied, but treating moonlight as a carried remainder rather than only a later body is inferred. **Reading change:** `تلاها` can be heard as following while preserving a luminous trace.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B008","root_001255/B001","root_001255/B003","root_001255/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_115c61bb3c8aac6b756b","text":"2. **Moonlight as derivative disclosure with ambivalence.** Lexical evidence: ق م ر B001 \"moon and its light\"; ق م ر B003 \"approaching or hunting by moonlight\"; ق م ر B009 \"sleeplessness in moonlight\"; ج ل و B008 \"searching gaze\" in 91:3. Structural cue: moon follows a feminine `ها`, then day unveils and night covers. Assigned role: moonlight is a secondary light that can guide, expose, hunt, and disturb. Abductive move: I infer that a reflected or following light can both reveal and make vulnerable; the packet supplies moonlight/hunting/sleeplessness and later searching gaze. Reading change: the ayah is no longer a simple night-sky image; it activates a watchful, derivative illumination that prepares for the soul's exposed inner knowledge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001255/B003","root_001255/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_26f1007d94a36b4c2345","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:2:1\",\"root_001255\",[\"B003\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ay ışığı, yaklaşmayı ve avlanmayı mümkün kılarken uykuyu da kaçırabilen, hem yol gösterici hem açığa çıkarıp huzursuz edici bir ışık hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B004","root_001033/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26f338dfba65dfa3d63e","text":"91:15 made تلا more important than قمر itself: ع ق ب B005/B006 turns the moon's following into the surah's first grammar of consequence. I was also surprised that ت ل و B004 liability after following resonated with ذ ن ب B006 and ع ق ب B010, making the moon's ordered following a quiet legal preview of retained consequence after the crime.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3603bde6c39d2f103713","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:2:3\",\"root_000186\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ayın güneşi izlemesi, eylemi sonucun izlemesine kadar uzanan düzenli ardışıklığın ilk kozmik örneğini kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B003","root_000186/B004","root_001033/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4d0260b440d120826d89","text":"3. **Remainder and liability after following.** Lexical evidence: ت ل و B003 \"remainder after what came before\"; ت ل و B004 \"liability or right that follows its holder\"; ذ ن ب B006 \"portion or allotted share\" in 91:14; ع ق ب B010 \"substitution and retained liability\" in 91:15. Structural cue: a following moon early in the oath-chain anticipates retained consequences after the denial/hamstringing narrative. Assigned role: تلا is read not only as spatial/temporal following but as residual claim. Abductive move: I supply the legal/obligational arrow from \"what follows\" to \"what remains owing\"; the packet supplies branch inventories of remainder, liability, sin/share, and outcome. Reading change: the moon's following becomes a quiet juridical grammar: what comes after carries claims from what preceded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001","root_001255/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_59e8f07f6a351e802054","text":"1. cue and role:** the moon is grammatically subordinated by `إذا تلاها` between the sun's exposure and the day/night reversal, so following assigns it a relay position. **Abductive move:** the order is explicit; construing that order as dependence within a light-cycle rather than mere chronology is inferred. **Reading change:** the moon reads as a successor carrying a prior luminous phase forward, not as an isolated object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B003","root_000186/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_96e15013bb2bb3e7cb81","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:2:3\",\"root_000186\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İzleme
